## Threshold audit and distribution readout

The threshold audit completes92 healthy trajectories and56 windows (14 distinct states,32 terminals). Independent audit verifies34,148 hashes, all native boundaries/features,48 frozen endpoints and every metric. Both mixed-text seeds classify only24/56 first-combat outcomes correctly (42.9%) from expected LP, despite LP MAE2.10/2.14. The exact scoped integer-rule forecast is56/56; joint disposition on the24 nonterminal windows is100%. At exact lethal1050, the learned mean1049.969 leaves artificial positive LP. Some LP1 zero-damage states receive tiny predicted losses on both sides and are classified as invalid simultaneous loss. No representation passes all threshold gates. These are14 deliberately selected threshold cases, not general match accuracy.

Preregister `effect-resolution-terminal-distribution-readout-2026-10-16` before implementation. Isolate expectation-before-threshold from distribution-before-threshold without updating parameters. The existing LP head already assigns probabilities to three exclusive damage-allocation atoms: self damage of the supplied gap, opponent damage of that gap, or no damage. Recover the three probabilities directly from the frozen logits (including the fixed zero logit). Require the original expected LP output, computed as negative first-two probabilities times the original normalized gap, to match every saved LP endpoint bit-for-bit. No rounding, snapping, tuned epsilon, learned-weight update or change to the magnitude/routing component.

Use four independently audited source panels: known-card confirmation288 windows, unseen attackers144, LP-boundary108 and exact-threshold56 (596 total recorded windows). Preserve each stored inference batch shape for exact reproduction. Evaluate all24 frozen LP heads (three architectures, four representations, two seeds) on all panels:96 panel-model endpoints, zero new native paths, fits or embeddings. Require original source identities, report/audit hashes, source prediction freezes and model checkpoints to verify. Carry previous failures forward explicitly.

The candidate readout pushes each atom probability through the terminal event, using only current public LP and the exact integer gap already supplied by the audited prospective rule component. A self-damage atom becomes self-loss iff gap>=self LP; an opponent-damage atom becomes self-win iff gap>=opponent LP; all remaining probability becomes continue. Preserve uncertainty by summing probabilities, rather than replacing allocation with a hard argmax. Score against native first-combat Win/continuation boundaries. This is a diagnostic interpretation of the current categorical output, restricted to the audited atom/magnitude scope; it does not validate arbitrary damage distributions, prevention effects, simultaneous losses or hidden information. The integer rule forecast remains an explicit reference, not a learned semantic achievement.

Retain the original mean-LP threshold classifier as baseline, including invalid simultaneous-loss errors. Freeze both readouts and original means before scoring. Report every panel, public state, LP level, position, recipient/outcome and threshold offset/family where present; duplicates are not independent samples. Require candidate terminal accuracy>=.95 and four-class Brier<=.02 in every nonempty stratum for both seeds of the primary mixed-text position-routed model. Keep all24 models and original failures visible. Report existing joint accuracy separately on native nonterminal states and leave all legacy destination/LP scores unchanged. This cannot retroactively pass previous gates or replace the production policy; it informs whether future training should supervise terminal outcome distributions rather than threshold expected damage.

Independent audit verifies75666 hashes,1536 full games/1546 main paths,16 preflight paths,768 action pairs,six cold replays and1524 invariants. Concrete encoding defect discovered during audit: PointObserver initializes EventHistory with scalar point.lp[0],and EventHistory initializes BOTH players tothat scalar. Old native outcomes remain reproducible outcomes ofthat encoder,not reliable evidence ofa correctly encoded LP comparison. ## Real-state action-head distillation preregistration

Before implementation, feature extraction, fitting or new outcomes, preregister `effect-resolution-optional-value-real-state-distillation-2026-10-16`. The window comparison does not establish a safer time gate: its reported 19 improvements versus sham are fewer than the unrestricted rule's 21, with zero harms in both on this panel. Keep unrestricted timing for this pilot; do not select winning trajectories, prompts, turns or model seeds. The objective is to test whether a small learned action head can reproduce useful altered-input teacher behavior while receiving only factual public observations. The teacher is a diagnostic distribution, not an oracle or a proven optimal action label.

Training source is ONLY the already audited online-confirmation sham panel (suffixes2026106001..6256): all recorded player0 observations from the corrected continuation boundary through its first disagreement, including nontriggering and ineligible records. Neither winners nor optional-action outcomes enter targets, weights, features or sampling. Deduplicate byte-identical observations for frozen feature computation while preserving every original occurrence in the sampling index. No new labels come from the window panel. ## Stage-specific action-value head training pilot preregistration

User steering: distinguish evidence needed to justify bounded exploratory training from evidence needed to claim stable benefit or replace the policy. Preserve all old protocols and failed gates. The frozen fresh-prefix coverage experiment continues unchanged. Before new fitting or evaluation, preregister `effect-resolution-action-value-head-pilot-2026-10-16`; require the fresh-coverage parent's complete independent audit before training. This is a small optimization/transfer canary with two fits, not a new card-text encoder or a production candidate.

Use all six deterministically selected parent cases and all20 legal state/action combinations, each with all32 future actor suffixes. These cases now become explicit development/training data for this follow-up. Do not use the earlier four outcome-selected action-value cases or any earlier exceptional winner labels. Parent selection includes four distinct encoded observations, two overlapping earlier exposed idle observations; no claim that training identities were blind. Group byte-identical observations with identical menus and masks. Average their action win-rate labels equally over the represented resource-world cases, then construct each seed's target distribution as normalize(parent-retention-probability × exp(mean conditional action value /0.25)), preserving exact zero mask holes. This is a regularized improvement target under the specified equal mixture of represented worlds, not an oracle for hidden game state. Weight each unique observation by its number of represented parent cases divided by six; use all four groups in every update.

Freeze the source trunk, encoder and architecture. Initialize fit seed2026108401 from retention head2026106701, and2026108402 from retention2026106702. Train only the existing ActionHead,256 fixed Adam updates at0.0001, with no checkpoint selection or hyperparameter search. Objective: full-group weighted KL(value target || current head) + weight1 KL(original factual low-LP policy || current head) + weight0.1 KL(initial retention head on high-LP data || current head). Reuse the audited low retention-features.pt (1429 unique states/9746 occurrences) and high training-features.pt (1119 unique/28433 occurrences), with fixed batch64 occurrence sampling from independent generators fit_seed+1000000 /fit_seed+2000000. Features and targets remain detached; no gradient or mutation reaches the trunk, source or initial heads. Keep both final endpoints even if they perform poorly.

Before fitting, independently recompute native-value targets, masks, all four frozen feature groups, dataset hashes and identity. Zero-update preflight checks real logits and masked gradients, verifies baseline retention initializations, and exercises synthetic equal-observation/different-world label aggregation. Reproduce a short optimizer sequence independently on a toy masked example, not an evaluation-selected actual endpoint. Log every loss component, parameter change and weighted KL/TV on all high/low occurrences. Independently replay both complete256-update fits after training and require exact tensors and loss curves before outcome collection. Freeze both new heads and all three evaluation comparators before starting new games.

Evaluate five arms: original factual policy, retention6701, value8401, retention6702, value8402. Every learned head runs continuously after the same repaired factual continuation boundary. Keep native code, core2026105000, opponent and all actual LP/history unchanged. Fixed evaluation grid: LP pairs(12001,2200),(12001,2400),(12001,2600),(3001,1800), both resource worlds, both initial optional choices,16 fresh shared actor suffixes2026108501–2026108516. The2400 LP stratum is absent from these value labels; low3001/1800 is a behavior-retention control, not globally unseen source training. No root observation or evaluation outcome enters fitting. All256 requests × five arms =1280 full games. Cold replay every arm at the first world/first suffix/decline of each LP pair (20 extra), plus four unchanged symmetric controls:1304 main paths. Setup replays four fixed parent harvesting requests exactly, one original/retention6701/retention6702 request at each LP pair with cold replay (24), plus four controls:32 native setup paths. None of the future evaluation seeds is run during setup; use the predeclared prefix seed2026108101 for setup only.

Report all gains/harms/ties and conditional paired intervals for each LP, world, initial choice and overall, with equal weight per predefined request, rather than requiring zero observed harms. Primary descriptive comparisons are each new head versus its matched retention head and versus original. Use16 shared suffix clusters,2000 bootstrap draws,seed2026108517. Report126 descriptive contrasts:six comparisons (four learned-minus-original plus two matched new-minus-retention) ×21 summaries (overall, plus all/world0/world1/decline/activate within each of four LP strata). These are diagnostics; the exploratory grade uses only the predeclared overall and low-LP means. Preserve every result and explicitly label the original2200/2600 fixtures as exposed development conditions.

Exploration grading is defined before outcomes: execution_valid requires native health, exact cold replay, factual inputs, masks, unchanged source/trunk and independent training reproduction. A promising expansion signal requires each seed's point-estimate overall net gain versus matched retention to be nonnegative, at least one strictly positive, and neither seed's low-LP mean return to drop more than0.02 absolute versus matched retention. Confidence intervals are reported but are not a prerequisite to this exploratory grade. If full-game differences are all zero while local training value improves without exceeding the low-LP tolerance, call the result inconclusive with local learning, not proven benefit or a failed implementation. Exceeding the low-LP tolerance is a regression concern to fix; no post-hoc threshold changes or extra samples. These grades authorize only subsequent bounded investigation; production promotion remains false for every outcome.

Budget:two fits/512 primary updates plus two exact audit replays,1280 new evaluation games/1304 main paths,32 native setup paths,1800-second service ceiling, live integrity progress,five-second heartbeat and persistent monitor. Data/target extraction and setup have no optimizer updates. Outcome-selected trace inspection finds low-LP regressions at original request111 and Winged request225: the first changed action at turn3/decision43 replaces summoning the resource with setting it. The single-disagreement branch then returns to original and still loses these sampled games. High cases46 and160 instead show value heads choosing summon where old heads choose set, following the same original prefix at turn3/decision41. These observations motivate conditional action-value measurement; they do not by themselves establish the expected-return ordering of the actions.

Frozen probability inspection adds an important distinction: in the low-LP Winged case, original summon/set/end probabilities are 86.6144%/8.1284%/5.2572%; value8401 gives 85.5143%/7.9595%/6.5262%, and value8402 gives 85.2819%/8.0758%/6.6423%. Both new heads reduce summon mass and increase end-phase mass while slightly reducing set mass. The paired uniform can nevertheless move from summon to set because the cumulative boundary shifts. Thus a sampled summon-to-set regression is not evidence that total set probability increased; evaluating all three actions is necessary. These numbers are descriptive frozen-input outputs, not fresh return estimates.

The separately preregistered next study is now launched on exactly those four exposed contexts. It reports every action return, 12 paired action contrasts, and 24 contrasts of frozen head probabilities weighted by those returns. Those quantities describe one decision with original continuation, not the value of running a learned head throughout. Cases are deliberately selected from gains/regressions; no unbiased generalization or pooled efficacy claim is made. No new fitting or checkpoint selection.
